  21|             F.H. Operation and maintenance plans. An operation and maintenance plan (O&M plan) is required at the
  22|                     time of application for all projects. The maintenance plan shall be designed to ensure compliance with
  23|                     the permit, this bylaw and that the Massachusetts Surface Water Quality Standards, 314 CMR 4.00 are
  24|                     met in all seasons and throughout the life of the system. The operation and maintenance plan shall
  25|                     remain on file with the SPGA and shall be an ongoing requirement. The O&M plan shall include:
  26|             (1) The name(s) of the owners) for all components of the system.
  27|             (2) Maintenance agreements that specify:
  28|                      (a) The names and addresses of the person(s) responsible for operation and maintenance.
  29|                     (b) The person(s) responsible for financing maintenance and emergency repairs.
  30|             (3) Maintenance schedule for all drainage structures, including swales and ponds.
  31|              (4) List of easements, with the purpose and location of each.
  32|                                                                                              182

===PAGE 183=== (ocr, 34 lines)
   1|                                                      SPECIAL TOWN MEETING - November 17, 2020
   2|             (5) The signature(s) of the owner(s).
   3|             (6) Stormwater management easements).
   4|                     (a) Stormwater management easements shall be provided by the property owner(s) as necessary for:
   5|                            [1] Access for facility inspections and maintenance.
   6|                            [2] Preservation of stormwater runoff conveyance, infiltration, and detention areas and facilities,
   7|                                   including flood routes for the one-hundred-year storm event.
   8|                            [3] Direct maintenance access by heavy equipment to structures requiring regular cleanout.
   9|                     (b) The purpose of each easement shall be specified in the maintenance agreement signed by the
  10|                            property owner.
  11|                     (c) Stormwater management easements are required for all areas used for off-site stormwater control,
  12|                            unless a waiver is granted by the SPGA.
  13|                    (d) Easements shall be recorded with the Worcester County Registry of Deeds prior to issuance of a
  14|                            certificate of completion by the SPGA.
  15|             (7) Changes to operation and maintenance plans.
  16|                   (a) The owner(s) of the stormwater management system must notify the SPGA of changes in
  17|                           ownership or assignment of financial responsibility.
  18|                   (b) The maintenance schedule in the maintenance agreement may be amended to achieve the purposes
  19|                           of this bylaw by mutual agreement of the SPGA and the responsible parties. Amendments must be
  20|                           in writing and signed by all responsible parties. Responsible parties shall include the owners),
  21|                           persons with financial responsibility, and persons with operational responsibility.
  22|             (8) Stormwater infrastructure shall be privately owned, inspected and maintained per the operation and
  23|                    maintenance plan approved for the project. Inspection and maintenance logs shall be provided to the
  24|                    Planning Board on a yearly basis by final day in June for the Town to use in preparation of its annual
  25|                    report to the EPA as part of the NPDES permit requirements.
  26|             (9) The O&M Plan shall include procedures for using dedicated funds, establishing an escrow account, and/
  27|                  or developing a maintenance contract, if determined appropriate to ensure adequate long-term
  28|                   maintenance.
  29|             (10 Stormwater Management operation and maintenance duties shall be recorded with the deed for each lot
  30|                    in a subdivision. The applicant may elect to setup a home owner's association (HOA) or other means to
  31|                    ensure all BMPs are inspected and maintained as required.
  32|             (11) Long-term operators responsible for O&M Plan implementation shall submit an annual report to the
  33|                    Planning Board documenting all inspection and maintenance completed on the stormwater system.
  34|                                                                                              183

===PAGE 184=== (ocr, 37 lines)
   1|                                                          SPECIAL TOWN MEETING - November 17, 2020
   2|              G.J. Surety. The SPGA may require the permittee to post, before the start of land disturbance or construction
   3|                      activity, a surety bond, cash, or other acceptable security. The form of the bond shall be approved by
   4|                      Town Counsel, and be in an amount deemed sufficient by the SPGA to ensure that the work will be
   5|                      completed in accordance with the permit. If the project is phased, the SPGA may release part of the
   6|                      bond as each phase is completed in compliance with the permit, but the bond may not be fully released
   7|                      until the SPGA has received the final inspection report as required by Subsection H J and issued a
   8|                      certificate of completion.
   9|              H.J. Inspections.
  10|              (1) At the discretion of the SPGA, periodic inspections of the stormwater management system construction
  11|                      may be conducted by the Town via the Planning Board, Conservation Commission, DPW or a
  12|                      professional engineer approved by the SPGA. Written reports shall include:
  13|                      (a) Inspection date and location.
  14|                       (b) Evaluation of compliance with the stormwater permit.
  15|                      (c) Any variations from approved specifications or any violations of the stormwater management
  16|                              plan.
  17|              (2) At a minimum, the SPGA or its designee may inspect the project site at the following stages:
  18|                      (a) Initial site inspection: prior to approval of any plan.
  19|                      (b) Erosion control inspection: to ensure erosion control practices are in accord with the filed plan.
  20|                      (c) Bury inspection: prior to backfilling of any underground drainage stormwater conveyance
  21|                              structures.
  22|                      (d) Final inspection. After the stormwater management system has been constructed and before the
  23|                              surety has been released, the applicant must submit a record plan detailing the actual stormwater
  24|                              management system as installed. The SPGA or its agent may inspect the system to confirm its "as-
  25|                              built" features. This inspector may also evaluate the effectiveness of the system in an actual storm.
  26|                              If the inspector finds the system to be adequate he shall so report to the SPGA, which will issue a
  27|                              certificate of completion. As-built plans shall be full-size plans that include all final grades,
  28|                              prepared by a professional engineer. All changes to project design should be clearly depicted on
  29|                              the as-built plans.
  30|                      (e) If the system is found to be inadequate by virtue of physical evidence of operational failure, even
  31|                              though it was built as called for in the stormwater management plan, it shall be corrected by the
  32|                              permittee before the performance guarantee is released. If the permittee fails to act, the Town of
  33|                              Lunenburg may use the surety bond to complete the work. If the system does not comply with the
  34|                              plan, the permittee shall be notified in writing of the violation and the required corrective actions.
  35|                              A stop-work order shall be issued until any violations are corrected and all work previously
  36|                              completed has received approval by the SPGA.
  37|                                                                                              184

===PAGE 185=== (ocr, 35 lines)
   1|                                                          SPECIAL TOWN MEETING - November 17, 2020
   2|              +K. Waivers.
   3|                      (1) The SPGA may waive strict compliance with any requirement of this bylaw or the rules and
   4|                              regulations promulgated hereunder, where such action:
   5|                             (a) Is allowed by federal, state and local statutes and/or regulations;
   6|                              (b) Is in the public interest; and
   7|                              (c) Is not inconsistent with the purpose and intent of this bylaw.
   8|                      (2) Any applicant may submit a written request to be granted such a waiver. Such a request shall be
   9|                              accompanied by an explanation or documentation supporting the waiver request and
  10|                              demonstrating that strict application of the bylaws does not further the purposes or objectives of
  11|                              this bylaw.
  12|                      (3) All waiver requests shall be discussed and voted on at the public hearing for the project.
  13|                      (4) If, in the SPGA's opinion, additional time or information is required for review of a waiver
  14|                              request, the SPA may continue the hearing to a date certain announced at the meeting. In the
  15|                              event the applicant objects to a continuance, or fails to provide requested information, the waiver
  16|                              request shall be denied.
  17|              J.L. Certificate of completion. The SPGA will issue a letter certifying completion upon receipt and approval
  18|                      of the final inspection reports and/or upon otherwise determining that all work of the permit has been
  19|                      satisfactorily completed in conformance with this bylaw.
  20|              M. Building Permits. The Building Commissioner shall issue a Building Permit without first confirming
  21|                      that and Stormwater Permit has been obtained or is otherwise not required for the project.
  22|              K.N. Enforcement.
  23|                     (1) Enforcement agents. The Building Commissioner, in conjunction with the Planning Board,
  24|                            Conservation Commission and DPW Director, shall enforce this bylaw, regulations, orders,
  25|                            violation notices, and enforcement orders, and may pursue all civil and criminal remedies for such
  26|                            violations.
  27|              LO. Civil relief. If a person violates the provisions of this bylaw, regulations, a permit, notice, or order
  28|                     issued thereunder, the Planning Board, through the Building Commissioner, may seek injunctive relief
  29|                      in a court of competent jurisdiction restraining the person from activities which would create further
  30|                      violations or compelling the person to perform abatement or remediation of the violation.
  31|              MP. Orders.
  32|                      (1) The Planning Board, in conjunction with the Conservation Commission and DPW Director, may
  33|                              issue a written order to the Building Commissioner to enforce the provisions of this bylaw or the
  34|                              regulations thereunder, which may include:
  35|                                                                                              185

===PAGE 186=== (ocr, 40 lines)
   1|                                                          SPECIAL TOWN MEETING - November 17, 2020
   2|                             (a) Elimination of illicit connections or discharges to the MS4;
   3|                              (b) Performance of monitoring, analyses, and reporting;
   4|                              (c) That unlawful discharges, practices, or operations shall cease and desist;
   5|                              (d) Remediation of contamination in connection therewith.
   6|                      (2) If the Building Commissioner, in conjunction with the Planning Board, Conservation Commission
   7|                              and DPW Director, determines that abatement or remediation of contamination is required, the
   8|                              order shall set forth a deadline by which such abatement or remediation must be completed. Said
   9|                              order shall further advise that, should the violator or property owner fail to abate or perform
  10|                              remediation within the specified deadline, the Town may, at its option, undertake such work, and
  11|                              expenses thereof shall be charged to the violator.
  12|                      (3) Within 30 days after completing all measures necessary to abate the violation or to perform
  13|                              remediation, the violator and the property owner will be notified of the cost incurred by the Town,
  14|                              including administrative costs. The violator or property owner may file a written protest objecting
  15|                              to the amount or basis of costs with the Planning Board within 30 days of receipt of the
  16|                              notification of the cost incurred. If the amount due is not received by the expiration of the time in
  17|                              which to file a protest or within 30 days following a decision of the Planning Board affirming or
  18|                              reducing the costs, or from a final decision of a court of competent jurisdiction, the costs shall
  19|                              become a special assessment against the property owner and shall constitute a lien on the owner's
  20|                              property for the amount of said costs. Interest shall begin to accrue on any unpaid costs at the
  21|                              statutory rate provided in MGL c. 59, § 57 after the 31st day at which the costs first become due.
  22|              N. Noncriminal disposition; right of entry.
  23|                      (1) As an alternative to criminal prosecution or civil action, the Planning Board may elect, through the
  24|                              Building Commissioner, to utilize the noncriminal disposition procedure set forth in MGL c. 40,
  25|                              § 21D, and Chapter 1, Article 1, of the General Bylaws of the Town, in which case the Planning
  26|                              Board, its authorized agents and the Building Commissioner of the Town shall be the enforcing
  27|                              person. The penalty for the first violation shall be $25. The penalty for the second violation shall
  28|                              be $50. The penalty for the third violation shall be $100 and $200 for the fourth and each berson. The penally for
  29|                              subsequent violation. Each day or part thereof that such violation occurs or continues shall
  30|                              constitute a separate offense.
  31|                      (2) Entry to perform duties under this bylaw. To the extent permitted by state law, or if authorized by
  32|                              the owner or other party in control of the property, the Planning Board, its agents, and employees
  33|                              may enter upon privately owned property for the purpose of performing their duties under this
  34|                              bylaw and regulations and may make or cause to be made such examination surveys or sampling
  35|                              as the Planning Board deems reasonably necessary.
  36|              OR. Appeals. The decisions or orders of the Planning Board, in conjunction with the Conservation
  37|                      Commission and DPW, shall be final. Further relief shall be to a court of competent jurisdiction.
  38|              PS. Remedies not exclusive. The remedies listed in this bylaw are not exclusive of any other remedies
  39|                      available under any applicable federal, state or local law.
  40|                                                                                              186

   3|                      regulations shall not invalidate any section, provision, paragraph, sentence, or clause thereof, nor shall
   4|                      it invalidate any permit or determination that previously has been issued.
